A check that fails on every line it is given is more likely to be the check than the chapter, and the number to distrust is the one that is exactly the size of the population it was run on.**

**Two instruments were wrong before they were believed and both were found rather than reported, which is item 173X and item 192's standing.** A holder-count script returned **zero for all six strings** and the cause was a glob written for `chapters/volume-NN.md/` against a layout of `chapters/volume-NN/`; rebuilt, it reproduces item 185's and item 190's figures exactly — **10 chapters across 3 volumes** and **0 chapters, 0 volumes** for the two twelve-word runs and **3 chapters across 1 volume** for the canon designation. **The twelve-word cross-file index then returned zero at seven and at six words, which is the signature of an index built at one window and queried at another**; rebuilt at each window it returns **253 distinct runs at seven and 540 at six**, against an index of 1,348,083 twelve-word grams, **so the three runs it reports at twelve words are a fact about the world and not a fault in the instrument.** **A zero that has not been checked against a setting known to return something is a scan broken in the direction of comfort, and that has now happened in this repair four times.**

**A per-chapter column was written into this phase's own record from memory and was wrong in four of the ten rows, and it is corrected where it stood.** The first version read `0401` 15/17/17/33 and so on, summed its sweep column to **249** against a headline of **240**, and explained the difference of nine as a de-duplication this method does not perform. **Measured, the four columns sum to 86, 110, 120 and 240 and the form column to 87, every one of them agreeing with its headline, because `sweep` counts occurrences and not distinct forms.** **The standing is that a per-chapter column computed by a different route than the headline is how a total and a column can both be internally consistent and jointly wrong, and writing a column from memory is that same failure with the measurement step left out.**

**Every gate re-run on the corrected tree and none carried forward.** `selftest` **PASS**. `calendar --volume 09` **50 files, 50 date lines parsed, 12 month-boundary restarts, 0 breaks inside a month, none unparsed**. `words --volume 09` **81,610**; `words` **1,466,199 in 620 files**; Volume 01 **202,117** unchanged. **98 `insert`, 590 `equal`, 0 `delete`, 0 `replace` against `b8780ff`, and 98 + 590 = 688 with the ten files holding 688 lines checked per file**, with the `equal` column derived as total − insert − delete so it does not depend on the context `git diff` is given, which is item 187's standing. **A word-level diff reports insertions, 0 deletions and 0 substitutions.** All ten date lines byte-identical to `b8780ff` **and on the line numbers they held at 7, 9, 7, 9, 7, 7, 9, 9, 9 and 7**, located and not by count, and `line 5` was never consulted because it is a date line in none of the ten. Section breaks **7 5 5 5 6 4 6 4 4 5**, bold markers **20 18 10 14 14 10 20 14 14 18**, question marks **0 on all ten**, quoted spans **166** and **333** quote characters identical span for span, titles and trailing newlines identical. `lifts` **ADDED 49 prose lines, 47 over six words, 25 over nine, mean 8.62** against **BASELINE 229, 187, 122, mean 11.44**. Re-print prose buckets **96, 376, 1,257 and 3,822**, longest runs unmoved at 147 in `chapter-0416.md` and 171 in `chapter-0417.md`, both in the range behind this one and neither touched.

**The four indices were all run and every hit ruled on, and one run is reported as a close call and deliberately kept.** `chapter-0408.md:51` against `:53` shares *and it is not hers and* at six words between the line that establishes whose the rail is and the line that uses that ownership to defeat an implication of blame, and **that is two different jobs done with one phrase and the sharing is the point.** Everything else is item 190's or item 182's classes: the canon designation `the man who comes in on the second of the two days` at **3 chapters across 1 volume**, which item 190's standing exempts from revoicing by name; the register connectives at 13, 21, 70, 142, 152, 154 and 200 chapters; and stock designations and durations below the threshold. The seven-word leaders reproduce the records above — `on the second of the two days` at **17 across 3** with the range excluded against item 185's 17, `a hand that was not his mother's` at **7 across 3** against item 182's 7 — **and that reproduction is the check that the six-word list is ruled the way those records ruled it.**

**The locks hold and were checked by name rather than by count.** Zero floor material, zero digits, zero instances of *seven* in words or numeral, zero question marks, zero bold markers, zero backticks and zero quote characters in the added prose, so the quoted-span gate could not have been broken by anything written here. No sentence begins with a lower-case `and`, `but` or `so`. The only capitalised tokens in the added prose are `Marn` and `Ottery`. **The four hundred and thirty miles, the nine miles and the four hundred yards of cold flags are each walked zero times**: the two walk-words are `chapter-0402.md:73`'s negation *neither of the two women had walked any of it* and `chapter-0408.md:31`'s *a walk four times a day for four years*, which is the courier's trade and is base, and the four hundred yards occurs once in the added prose as a locative and not as a journey. **The separations hold and were checked with item 191's regex and not the one that misleads**: `grep -nE 'woman of twenty([^0-9-]|$)'` returns nothing over the ten files and the looser `woman of twenty\b` returns the clerk of twenty-four, because a hyphen is a word boundary. **Neither line this pass wrote names any of the three separated people or places any person in a room.**

**The canon conflicts are carried and this phase settled none and touched none of the lines they sit on.** **The stair conflict is five chapters deep — `chapter-0406.md:3` against `chapter-0404.md:23`, `chapter-0409.md:15`, `chapter-0417.md:15` and `chapter-0425.md:43`, with `chapter-0399.md:9` against `:17` one volume back — and this pass wrote no sentence about which stair any room is on, and neither of its two edited lines is in the rented house.** Also carried and untouched: `chapter-0398.md:31` against `chapter-0395.md:7` and `:5`; `chapter-0397.md:3` against `:21`, `:53`, `:63` and `:65`; `chapter-0375.md:5` against `chapter-0356.md` with the counting room's sixteen books; `chapter-0378.md:27`; and `chapter-0415.md:3` against `chapter-0419.md:3`. **The two people in their boxes stay in them, the question stays a question, the figure at the end of the cold passage is not handed on and gets no heading, the name at the end of a struck line is not printed and neither is the name of the hand that struck it, and nobody was thanked, forgiven or sent for.** No canon figure was changed to make a sentence read better and no figure was invented; the two sentences written contain two years, eleven years, a fortnight and four hundred yards, all already on the page.

**What is left is most of it and no phase may imply otherwise. One hundred and ninety-seven chapters of six hundred and twenty have been through the prose repair, thirty-two per cent, and this phase repaired none of them new and took two paragraphs out of ten already repaired and audited.** Volume 08 is repaired from 0351 to 0399, Volume 11 from 0501 to 0549, Volume 12 from 0551 to 0619, and **Volume 09 from 0401 to 0430, three fifths of it.** **Volume 10 is untouched in its entirety and is the only volume in this manuscript where no repair has been done at all, and it is fifty chapters. Four hundred and twenty-three chapters are unrepaired, sixty-eight per cent, and every one of them is in Volumes 01 to 10. This repair at this rate is forty-three phases across the manuscript and eight across the eighty chapters of Volumes 09 and 10 that are still unrepaired, of which all eight are after this one, and that is a person's decision and not a phase's.** The next range is `chapter-0431.md` to `chapter-0440.md` and its prompt exists at `workspace/prose-repair-0021/PROMPT.md`, which this phase did not create.

**Four debts are outstanding and no pass in this repair touched them, and they are owed elsewhere and not here.**
